- FIX 1 (stage-sidecar.mjs): the staging install now runs `pnpm install --prod --config.node-linker=hoisted` — a classic npm-style tree of REAL directories, zero links. Empirically verified with BOTH pnpm 11.24 (local) and the exact CI pnpm 11.22.0: zero non-.bin symlinks, .pnpm reduced to a metadata-only lock.yaml. A yaml-only `nodeLinker: hoisted` key in pnpm-workspace.yaml is IGNORED by pnpm 11.x (verified — the first staging attempt failed the new gate) — the CLI flag is load-bearing; the yaml line stays as documentation. node_modules/.bin is deleted post-install (POSIX bin shims are symlinks; the runtime tree never spawns bins). Staged tree: 301.5 MB → 124.6 MB (83.3 MB after win32-x64 prebuild pruning).
- FIX 2 (stage-sidecar.mjs): THE ZERO-LINKS GATE — walks the entire staged tree, fails the stage on ANY symlink/junction (Node lstatSync reports Windows junctions as symlinks — one walk covers both OSes), plus refuses a package-bearing .pnpm. It caught the yaml-only regression mid-development and the win32 CI run printed "zero-links gate passed".
- FIX 3 (release.yml): THE WINDOWS PRE-PACK BOOT GATE — after staging + node.exe bundling, BEFORE `pnpm tauri build`: Start-Process the real staged node.exe against app\dist\main.js with the same env the Rust shell passes (ACUTE_TOKEN/ACUTE_DB_PATH, cwd = the app dir), poll for the ACUTE_READY handshake (90s), on failure print the engine's stdout/stderr tails + fail the release (NSIS build skipped). Would have caught R53's stale port, R54's silent one-shot, R55's EISDIR, and R57's link farm — every packaged-engine failure to date — before any reached the owner.
- Gate war story (honest): the gate's FIRST run (33409164781) failed on its own script bug — a RELATIVE ArgumentList resolved against the child's WorkingDirectory ('...sidecar\app\app\dist\main.js'); the SECOND run (33409487334) failed on the same class with a repo-relative path ('<wd>\src-tauri\staging\...'). Both times the gate did its job: engine stderr surfaced in full, NSIS build skipped, nothing packed, no draft release. Fix: ALL Start-Process paths ABSOLUTE via Join-Path $PWD. Run 3 (33409868288): "R57 boot gate: staged engine is UP on Windows — ACUTE_READY {\"port\":64511}".
- Also fixed pre-commit: .gitignore's old 3-line staging pattern un-ignored the sidecar/ DIRECTORY itself (re-including everything inside) — git add -A with a locally staged tree would sweep 124 MB of node_modules into the index (caught in time). New pattern ignores sidecar/* contents except the README placeholder. And the staging script's placeholder README write is now byte-identical to the committed README (R51-R56 wrote a 3-line variant — every local stage dirtied the tree).
- Gates: lint clean, typecheck clean (root + agent-core), 1071/1071 in 76 files (no product code changed — packaging only), version:check 0.57.0 ×4, docs:check 148/0 (the sandbox's 3 unreachable-URL WARNs are raw.githubusercontent 429 rate-limits on sandbox network — CI's clean network is the authority). Dev stack booted as sanity: vite 200, sidecar ACUTE_READY + /health 200 + /agents 200.
- Docs: CHANGELOG 0.57.0; round-57.md NEW (full post-mortem incl. the audit-table miss + the gate war story); AGENT-MEMORY lesson #70 ("a green gate that verifies the wrong platform is worse than no gate — it launders the failure into 'proven'"); TESTING.md R57 block; HANDOFF header; status.json → 0.57.0/round 57; ui-iterations README board caught up (rounds 51-56 one-liners + round-57 row).
- Pushed 1fa18d7 → CI 33407826049 GREEN. Tag v0.57.0 (moved twice through the gate fixes → final 7b17f02) → Release 33409868288 GREEN (launcher-kit + desktop-installer + github-release), installer ACUTE-CODE_0.57.0_x64-setup.exe 35.3 MB; draft PUBLISHED as "ACUTE-CODE 0.57.0 — The Engine-Bundling Round" after API verification. CI on final tip 33409870419 GREEN.
- DASHBOARD truth-sync ab92309: version 0.57.0, engine-bundling-fix feature card (package icon), R57 milestone, ciNote with BOTH verified run IDs, usage data intentionally unchanged (packaging round — no new agent turns; the R56 snapshot is the latest truth). Build + Pages deploy green.

The packaged engine's dependencies finally survive the installer: the staged tree is real directories (hoisted linker), a zero-links gate makes link-regression a build failure, and the release boots the engine ON WINDOWS before packing it — the first Windows-boot-verified engine in the product's history (ACUTE_READY port 64511 on the runner).

Task ID: R63
Agent: orchestrator (main)
Task: Round 63 — the desktop-update round (owner: "the desktop application was not reinstalled properly, not updated properly… whenever I run the acute.bat file… it will check the application version, then it will properly update the application on my desktop. If it needs to delete it completely, then it will delete it completely and reinstall it if needed").

Work Log:
- Diagnosis from the ground up: the GitHub release list's newest installer asset was v0.60.0 — R61 and R62 bumped versions and pushed commits but NEVER pushed their v* tags, and release.yml only builds the installer on a tag push. The launcher was correctly saying "0.60.0 is current" (nothing newer existed) while the site/dev flow served the tip — the exact "features added to the site but the desktop app not updated" report, and the reason desktop-only features (computer use, the embedded browser) were absent for the owner.
- Launcher hardening (launcher/acute_launcher.py, flagged per golden rule 1): the version-TRUTH chain — every desktop launch compares the newest release against the uninstall REGISTRY version, the installed EXE's real FileVersion on disk (new PowerShell VersionInfo probe), and after launch the running ENGINE's /health version (captured from the sidecar's listening line; token-free probe). A hybrid install (registry bumped, exe stale — the silent-install-over-a-closing-app leftover), missing files, a sticky repair flag, or ACUTE.bat reinstall triggers a FULL removal: stop + poll for process exit (not the R54 fixed sleep) → NSIS uninstaller run synchronously (_?= pins it in place) → rd /s /q residue sweep → stale uninstall-registry entries dropped → fresh install → same verification; one full retry on verify failure; honest site-flow fallback + a sticky repair flag that self-heals the next run. Installer downloads sha256-verified against the GitHub asset digest (one retry). New commands: ACUTE.bat reinstall / ACUTE.bat uninstall (clean removal, no credentials needed, %APPDATA% data always kept); status shows registry version, exe-on-disk version (+ HYBRID warning), latest release + UPDATE PENDING, repair-flag state; the version-check panel prints every signal before any decision; the site plan panel states plainly that the embedded browser + computer use are desktop-only.
- agent-core: /health's VERSION derives at boot from the package.json next to the compiled code (staged verbatim into the installer) — it was a hardcoded "0.3.0" for 60+ rounds. server.test.ts pins VERSION == package.json version + semver shape.
- Launcher/ACUTE.bat header docs updated for the new commands (CRLF preserved — 77 CRLF lines, file(1) verified); launcher/README.md documents the verification model.
- Process fix (the real root cause): docs/runbooks/MAINTENANCE.md recipe g — "Shipping a release — THE TAG IS PART OF THE ROUND": bump → verify → commit → PUSH THE TAG → watch the workflow → verify the draft release carries the installer. A version bump without its tag has shipped nothing to the desktop.
- Docs: round-63.md (new), CHANGELOG 0.63.0, HANDOFF header, docs/README index row, stamps refreshed; docs:check 158/0.
- Verification: pnpm lint CLEAN, typecheck CLEAN, root 1542/1542 (104 files), agent-core 801/801 (48 files), version:check 0.63.0 ×4, py_compile + pure-function tests + a 12-scenario desktop_flow simulation battery (current / hybrid / upgrade / newer-kept / forced-reinstall / repair-flag self-heal / engine-mismatch → flag / missing-files / first-install / post-install verify retry / unrepairable fallback / the real _desktop_uninstall against a real temp dir) + a live `status` smoke run on Linux.
- THIS round ships its tag: v0.63.0 pushed → release.yml builds the installer (R57 boot gate) → draft release with ACUTE-CODE_0.63.0_x64-setup.exe verified via the API before close-out.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause fixed (the missing release pipeline step) + the launcher now PROVES the desktop app is current instead of trusting one registry string; /health tells the truth; reinstall/uninstall commands shipped.
- Honest caveat: the launcher's Windows-only paths are code-complete + simulation-verified but live-verify on the owner's machine (headless Linux sandbox); the first ACUTE.bat app run after 0.63.0 IS that verification, with ACUTE.bat reinstall as the nuclear option and status showing the version truth.
- ACUTE-CODE @ (this commit) v0.63.0, tagged + release verified; suites 1542 root / 801 agent-core.

Work Log:
- 4 parallel Explore agents mapped every owner error string to a root cause: (1) browser navigate sent NO SSE frame and the panel's 4s poll ADOPTED the server URL on a fresh tab without creating the WebView2 (the blank-panel-until-manual-Enter bug + "no native webview for tab"); (2) WebView2's ExecuteScriptAsync returns eval results JSON-encoded while our scripts return JSON.stringify(...) — the callback string was DOUBLE-encoded, the single parse yielded a string, data.ok was undefined → "the page rejected the script" on EVERY click/type/eval on real Windows (sandbox mocks single-encode — why every test passed); (3) browserActiveTabSessionId() was a process-global LRU + a shared cookie profile + never-cleaned webviews (the cross-session leak); (4) dropped/pasted image bytes were discarded client-side and renderAttachments rendered a.name only (the "Image not found" failure); (5) PowerShell capsules rode stdin under -Command - (exit-0-empty + EPIPE failure modes) with a cold csc Add-Type; (6) the Windows key tool typed literal text (key "tab" → t-a-b); (7) the monitor's 6s decay closed the indicator while the agent thought.
- Wave 1 (parallel): R67-A attachments (POST /attachments/upload, composer drop/paste/ingest, renderAttachments teaches analyze_image(path)); R67-B chat UX (DebugReportCard copy + auto-collapse; the debug-gated full-conversation copy); R67-C computer robustness (-EncodedCommand capsules, the SendKeys table + chords + focused readback, probe fallback, turn-holds in the monitor store). The orchestrator did the browser core inline: the chat-session→tab binding (bind route + ag-<chatSession> minting + browser-open frame + openBrowserForChatSession + scoped get_state), the instant browser-navigate frame + create-on-adopt + the agentNavSeq effect, parseWebViewEvalJson (the double-encoding normalizer), the bind call in runTurn, per-project cookie profiles, and the instant Tauri sidebar width.
- Wave 2 (parallel): R67-D screenshot thumbnails (raster-cache LRU/TTL + the raster route + the screenshot SSE frame + LiveTurn.screenshots + ScreenshotStrip); R67-E prompts/skills (browser-first discipline, the Tab-walk, image-attachment guidance, tool descriptions, golden fixture regenerated).
- Live battery on the rebuilt dist: /health; POST /browser/bind 200 + honest 400; the raster route's honest 404 (the ephemeral message) + 401 without auth; POST /attachments/upload → the file ON DISK at attachments/<name> + the -2 dedupe suffix.
- R67-F (docs): round-67.md (the verbatim directive map), CHANGELOG 0.67.0, HANDOFF refresh, EMBEDDED-BROWSER/COMPUTER-USE/DEBUG-MODE R67 sections, NEW ATTACHMENTS runbook, IMPLEMENTED-API ROUND-67 (3 new routes + 3 new SSE frames), TESTING, both indexes. docs:check 165/0/0 after re-stamping to the environment's date. One drift resolved in the code's favor (plugin 21 / server 25 measured vs the sub-agent's 22/24 claim).

R68-0 root-caused every failure cluster at source before a line was written (the stalled screenshot loop, the frame_stale chains, the capability_fail_closed input deaths, the Edge frontmost churn, the sparse accessibility trees, the vision 429 stalls).
- R68-A: the screenshot sideband frame became a {type:"screenshot"} WorkingEntry APPENDED to the open liveTurn's working array at frame-arrival order (the position already encodes the capture moment) and rendered INLINE between the tool rows by the rewritten ScreenshotRow.tsx (lazy raster fetch, the honest "expired" placeholder, click → dialog); LiveTurn.screenshots, the cap-8 sidecar array and ScreenshotStrip.tsx are GONE; the debug full-turn export carries the [screenshot captured by <tool>] marker.
- R68-B: SetWindowDisplayAffinity(hwnd, WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE 0x11) via raw FFI in mini.rs at both open sites — the monitor stays fully rendered on the owner's physical display while dropping out of EVERY capture API (GDI CopyFromScreen, Windows.Graphics.Capture, OBS, screen share), so captures show what is BEHIND the bar and vision never reads the indicator again; the constant is pinned by a cross-platform cargo test; pre-Win10-2004 hosts fail the call silently (non-fatal, documented at source).
- R68-C: C1 Windows.Forms/SendKeys is a DEAD input dependency (LoadWithPartialName failed on the owner's modern .NET while the captures' own Add-Type worked — the live trace proved it) → raw SendInput: KEYEVENTF_UNICODE per-char text (no escaping class of bugs at all), VK chords incl. the win key for the first time, the ARGV ceiling guard (31,875 measured chars, refuses BEFORE the spawn with a self-teaching error); C2 withForegroundRetry (a frontmost mismatch auto-activates and retries ONCE); C3 the AttachThreadInput → minimize/restore activation escalation; C4 PokeChromium (WM_GETOBJECT to every Chrome_RenderWidgetHostHWND child + 400ms settle + sparse retry) so Edge's web accessibility tree becomes searchable by find_elements; C5 MAX_FRAME_AGE_MS 10s→30s (the vision roundtrip itself was 10–25s); C6 the vision relay retries 429/5xx ([1500,3000] backoff, both wire formats); C7 the prompts, skill and tool descriptions teach the new truth.
- Verification: root 2003/2003 in 122 files (agent-core 1101/59, frontend 890/61, e2e 12/12 re-run); lint/typecheck/build clean; version:check 0.68.0 ×4; docs:check 0 failures proven with the clock pinned to the round date (165 docs — the sandbox clock read 2026-09-05 while round-68 is dated 2026-09-06, the future-date sanity quirk); git-status audit confirmed ZERO browser files touched.

Task ID: R69-CLOSEOUT
Agent: orchestrator (main)
Task: Round 69 — the computer-use enforcement layer: every mutating action returns an observation receipt, clicks verify what they hit, and the re-capture loops die (no new owner report — the residuals R68's own close-out named).

Work Log:
- The problem left standing: action_sent:true meant MAYBE, and the only verification read cost a 5–25s screenshot+vision round; a stale frame hard-failed frame_stale whose honest recovery was ANOTHER capture; the model re-observed after every action because nothing told it what changed. R69 makes the R68 mechanisms automatic so the agent stops re-capturing and starts knowing.
- NEW framehash.ts (pngjs@7.0.0 MIT — the round's only new prod dependency, license audit CLEAN at 134 deps): 8×8 aHash over Rec.601 grayscale + hamming, region hashes, a 4-entry decode LRU; frames gain aHash + provenance ("model"|"auto_refresh"|"observation") computed at registration.
- All 11 mutating tools attach an observation after a 600ms settle — {frameId, screenChanged (full-frame hamming >4), focusedElementName, activeApp, titleChanged} with returnState compact|none|full — attached BEFORE the audit-journal append (the journal records the receipt the model actually got); the post-action frame renders INLINE in the chat through the R68 capture-moment pipe; coordinate clicks upgrade blind "unverified" → "changed"|"unchanged" and carry hitElementName; wait() reports what changed; element middle/right clicks route without coordinate guessing.
- The dead-ends die: a coordinate action on a >30s frame auto-refreshes first (full hamming ≤8 → proceed; target-region ≤6 → proceed; else the NEW frame_changed refusal carrying the ALREADY-registered fresh frame — one zoom round-trip, not a screenshot+replan cycle); and the 3rd consecutive near-identical model capture refuses screen_unchanged with the three productive alternatives (act / wait() / find_elements) — nothing registers, so the loop cannot feed itself.
- Windows fixes: the scroll tool was QUADRATIC (N wheel events each carrying N×120 — a 10-tick scroll moved 100× the intent; now ONE event, horizontal rides real MOUSEEVENTF_HWHEEL); long type >300 chars rides a stdin base64 clipboard paste (the 32,767 CreateProcess ceiling bypassed); the poke is dual-object (OBJID_CLIENT + UiaRootObjectId) with a sparse-poll and --force-renderer-accessibility launches; the monitor stops stealing focus (tao's set_focus synthesizes an ALT-key SendInput pair — deleted; WS_EX_NOACTIVATE via raw FFI with a zero-read guard).
- Verification: root 2082/2082 in 123 files (agent-core 1180/60; frontend 890/61 unchanged — zero frontend files; e2e 12/12); lint/typecheck/build clean; cargo check --target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc clean (plain AND --tests, zero warnings from mini.rs); version:check 0.69.0 ×4; docs:check 0 after re-stamping eight R68-future-dated files to the live clock.
- Close-out: commit b8254f6 (35 files, +4,018/−304) → TAG v0.69.0 pushed on b8254f6 → the release workflow built both assets and the close-out PUBLISHED it (the publish-at-close-out ritual begins here — the step the R63–R67 drafts never got, which R74's root-cause hunt would later turn into the freeze mechanism); DASHBOARD truth-synced to 0.69.0 at 801f44c (the state the R70 restore pass verified as the exact released state). Receipt note, honest: a later sandbox reset ate the R67–R69 sandbox-worklog entries, so this round's CI-run and release ids were lost with them — the tag mapping (v0.69.0 → b8254f6, 2026-09-05 22:46) and the published release list are the durable receipts.

Stage Summary:

Work Log:
- The report read precisely: the git-pull half worked (the owner's repo sat at the 0.73.0 tip), the desktop-update half silently no-op'd, and "as it used to" dated the freeze between the 0.67 and 0.73 eras. R74-a's empirical proof against the live API: GitHub's /releases list sorts never-published DRAFTS above every published release — the five R63–R67 drafts sat at list positions 1–5, and _desktop_latest_release's first-match walk (per_page=20, the repo at exactly 20/20 releases) returned draft v0.67.0 as "latest" → registry, exe and release all "agreed" on 0.67.0 → the decision ladder correctly launched with no error, nothing to see (and it "used to work" because in the 0.63–0.67 era ALL releases were drafts).
- The fix (the first launcher change since R63, flagged per golden rule 1; ACUTE.bat BYTE-IDENTICAL, CRLF verified): NEW pure _pick_latest_release — walks EVERY entry, parses versions from matching installer assets, returns the numerically greatest (integer tuples: 0.10.0 > 0.9.0, 1.0.0 > 0.99.99); drafts stay first-class (the owner's PAT sees them — a fresh draft installs on the next double-click, the recipe-g contract); a same-version tie prefers the published entry; malformed entries skipped, never raised. _desktop_latest_release rewritten onto it (per_page 20→100, one transient retry, never-raise/dev-fallback byte-identical); the version-truth panel now NAMES the tag it picked — the freeze is VISIBLE, not silent; ACUTE.bat status annotates published/draft.
- NEW launcher/tests/test_pick_latest_release.py — the repo's FIRST Python suite (stdlib unittest, zero deps): 10 tests pinning the picker's contract incl. the EXACT owner-freeze list shape (the real 2026-09-07 API order, five drafts above the published releases → must pick 0.73.0 with the chosen asset's id+digest passthrough — the sha256 install verification depends on it), order immunity both directions, the lexicographic trap, the all-drafts era, tie-prefers-published, malformed-never-crash — wired into BOTH ci.yml's verify job and release.yml's launcher-kit gate (github-release needs the kit job, so every tag is test-gated); the kit assembly copies seven named files only — tests cannot leak into the shipped zip.
- Out-of-band remediation: the five stale drafts PUBLISHED via the API (releases 381461843 / 381577121 / 382282125 / 383173421 / 383247624, all HTTP 200 — non-destructive, reversible), which also un-freezes any install still running the pre-fix launcher (the settled list carries v0.73.0 first, so even the OLD first-match walk returns "upgrading 0.67.0 → 0.73.0").
- R74-b hostile independent verification: 9/9 PASS, verdict SHIP — diff review (the decision ladder/download/install/self-update untouched), tests, py_compile, the LIVE smoke (the real /releases JSON through the picker → 0.73.0, run twice — the exact data that froze the owner), ACUTE.bat byte-identity, the kit-leak check, YAML validity, the old-launcher downgrade simulation across all three list states (no state of the world can take the owner's machine below 0.67.0), and the self-update delivery proof (the kit's launcher blob differs from the fixed repo file → copy + os.execve re-exec fires on the next double-click).
